# ES — Spain sights: individual worldwide reviews

- [Worldwide v2](../../../rules.md) · [Workflow](../../future-country-workflow.md)
- Initial editorial inventory: 50 UNESCO inscriptions, 35 tentative nominations and 16 established national parks at property level, including the Balearic and Canary islands. Mar de las Calmas remains proposed, not counted ([draft law consultation](https://www.miteco.gob.es/en/parques-nacionales-oapn/participacion-publica/info-anteproyecto-mar-calmas-2025.html)). All seven Gaudí components represented separately, with the entire Sagrada Família visitor experience distinct from the listed façade and crypt. Altamira original series and replica separate. All GPS approximate; other serial components selectively covered. Broad peer groups provisional; four city and two palace/fortress profiles checked against current YAML; others from recent repository inspections. Source links confirm limited identity/context only, not all material claims. No perfect-ten anchors added.
